# Lines outside the generator's line space, REAL audio

Check: `tests/synthetic_eval.off_distribution` (exact membership in the sampled support of generate.py's `deliberate_sound`, `air_hiss` and `junk_sound` via `tests/finetune_ref.py`; the reason is named by the same hand rules as in README "Schema / line-format issues"). This is the check behind the synthetic "193 of 1181 lines" figure. Every line counted here parsed strictly; it is valid vocabulary in a combination the training data never contains. Counts are over all conditions a dataset was run in (as recorded and 20/10/5 dB for positives), both splits.

## Config: frozen

61708 of 160141 emitted lines (38.5 %) are outside generate.py's line space (synthetic run: 193 of 1181, 16.3 %). 808 distinct lines.

### By reason

| reason | count | share of OOD |
|---|---|---|
| hum over 1 s that is not a deliberate flat hum | 30192 | 48.9 % |
| other unseen combination | 7320 | 11.9 % |
| hum line sounds like background noise | 6769 | 11.0 % |
| very short hiss | 6683 | 10.8 % |
| very short hum that sounds like talking | 3343 | 5.4 % |
| very short hum that sounds like whistle | 2627 | 4.3 % |
| non-flat hum with small pitch change that sounds like talking | 2001 | 3.2 % |
| loud background / long hiss (generate.py makes those quiet or normal) | 1282 | 2.1 % |
| non-flat hum with small pitch change that sounds like whistle | 1087 | 1.8 % |
| very short hum that sounds like coughing | 247 | 0.4 % |
| non-flat hum with small pitch change that sounds like laughing | 97 | 0.2 % |
| non-flat hum with small pitch change that sounds like coughing | 59 | 0.1 % |
| non-flat hum with small pitch change that sounds like background music | 1 | 0.0 % |
